Phone Full/Transpose pagination — Hymn 223

Resolved by Hymns (1985), page 223: song-138d4697-d2d9-414a-885c-82b760f1f371. Baseline ce6d50e. Phone CSS viewports 390×844 and 430×932; tablet control 820×1180. Chromium/Edge and Windows WebKit are simulations, not physical iPhone Safari.

Root cause

The exact physical 11-page state was not reproduced. Seven-page phone expansion was reproduced at both widths in both engines, with and without chords. Continuous was also excessively long. This is candidate fitting/engraving, not oversized measured systems or forced virtual page breaks. End-of-system Full lyrics overhang the SVG: at zoom .62, “the” reaches 593.21 against width 587.10; “helps” reaches 596.14. Clipping checks reject these compact candidates. Full lacked the measured lyric-margin correction already used by Melody. At 430px a clean three-page candidate was also rejected because 10.9999997507px was less than the 11px threshold due to floating-point noise. The two-page PDF target is known and passed correctly; no safe candidate reaches it.

General phone-only correction

Phone Full reserves measured lyric overhang plus a small clearance and re-engraves, with at most three retries and a margin reset for every zoom/key/profile trial. It reuses the Lead margin strategy without Lead tail balancing. Only phone Full gets a 0.000001px numeric tolerance at the unchanged 11px readability floor. The .55 notation floor remains unchanged. No blanket zoom reduction, song-specific hint, chord modification, page-planner change, PDF change or Melody change. Tablet keeps the original rendering path. Compact candidates can qualify safely; the existing chooser minimizes pages then prefers the largest safe size.

Results

Original PDF is two pages. Full original key: 7→4 at 390 in both engines; 7→4 at 430 Chromium, 7→3 WebKit. Without chords, the before result is still seven; after correction WebKit gives three at both widths. Chords contribute normal ink height and can add a page but do not cause the seven-page expansion. Melody remains two pages, including +2.

Full original/+2/−2: Chromium 390 = 4/4/4; 430 = 4/3/3. WebKit 390 = 4/4/4; 430 = 3/3/3. Before Chromium = 7/7/7 at each width. Selected phone Full lyrics measure 11–12px, above the established floor within numerical tolerance. Physical readability judgment is still required.

Continuous original-key height falls from 4268→2062px at 390 and 4687→2404px at 430 in Chromium. WebKit after is 2348px/1873px. Engraved system count falls from 21 to 12/14 in Chromium and 14/11 in WebKit. The correction reduces excessively fragmented horizontal reflow.

390 Chromium page 1 changes from three systems/three source measures to three systems/six measures. Used height changes 534→466 of 631px: absolute blank space does not always shrink as notation gets compact, but each page carries more music and total pages fall. 430 page 1 grows from three to four systems and 581→642 of 719px used. No corrected page consists of one tiny system. The unchanged minimum-page planner balances remaining whitespace; some systems could move earlier without reducing total pages. Per-page reasons below make that distinction explicit.

Controls

The four named controls retain phone page counts. Zoom changes by .01 for #68 at 390 and #35 at 430 as edge clearance affects reflow; no readability-floor change. Additional #144 improves 3→2 at 430. Short “Thanks to Our Father” stays one page at both widths (.66/.73). At 820, every tested system bound/page assignment/zoom/profile matches exactly, including #223 in all three keys and the four named controls. Melody geometry/zoom/pages also match exactly in Chromium. Existing tablet #223 +2 quality warnings remain unchanged.
